## Geometry · 屬性

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-22

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#properties

**parameter**　length

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- length （只讀）
- 線狀圖徵的長度。 該計算將使用 2D 笛卡爾算法。 對于點和多點幾何，長度為零。 對于面幾何，長度將是邊界的 2D 長度。
- Double

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#properties

## Geometry · 屬性

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-23

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　length3D

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- length3D （只讀）
- 線狀圖徵的 3D 長度。 該計算將使用 3D 笛卡爾算法。 對于點和多點幾何，長度為零。 對于面幾何，長度將是邊界的 3D 長度。 注意： 該操作不執行水平和垂直座標系之間的單位轉換。 假設 x、y 和 z 座標在同一線性單位中。 注意： 此屬性僅適用于投影資料。
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#properties

## Geometry · angleAndDistanceTo(other, {method})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-31

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-angleAndDistanceTo

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method （可選）
- Specifies the method that will be used to measure distance. GEODESIC—The shortest line between any two points on the earth's surface on a spheroid (ellipsoid). One use for a geodesic line is to determine the shortest distance between two cities for an airplane's flight path. This is also known as a great circle line if based on a sphere rather than an ellipsoid. GREAT_ELLIPTIC—The line on a spheroid (ellipsoid) defined by the intersection at the surface by a plane that passes through the center of the spheroid and the start and endpoints of a segment. This is also known as a great circle when a sphere is used. LOXODROME—The line follows a single compass bearing, or azimuth. Great circle routes are often broken into a series of loxodromes, which simplifies navigation. This is also known as a rhumb line. PLANAR—Planar measurements use 2D Cartesian mathematics to calculate lengths and areas. This option is only available when measuring in a projected coordinate system and the 2D plane of that coordinate system will be used as the basis for the measurements. Planar measurements reflect the projection of geographic data onto a 2D surface, and do not account for the curvature of the earth. (默認值為 GEODESIC)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-angleAndDistanceTo

## Geometry · crosses(second_geometry)

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-47

**text**　指定兩個幾何是否相交于較小形狀類型的幾何。
如果兩條折線僅共用公共點（至少有一個點不是端點），則這兩條折線交叉。 如果折線和面在面的內部共享一條折線（不等于整條折線）或一個公共點（對于垂直線），那么該折線與面交叉。
本圖僅顯示 True 關系。

## Geometry · cut(cutter)

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-50

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-cut

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　將該幾何分割到剪切折線的左右兩側。
剪切折線或面時，將在其與剪切折線相交的位置進行分割。 每一塊都被分類為剪切線的左側或右側。 此分類基于剪切線的方向。 目標折線中不與剪切折線相交的部分將作為輸入折線結果右側的部分返回。 如果未對幾何進行剪切，則左側幾何將為空 (None)。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-cut

## Geometry · densify(method, distance, {deviation})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-54

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-densify

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method
- The method of densification. DISTANCE—Creates a feature that is a piecewise linear approximation of the input. ANGLE—Creates a feature that is a piecewise linear approximation of the input. Vertices are introduced at points where the angle between tangents at those points is the provided angle. GEODESIC—Densifies and reshapes segments between input vertices so that the output segments follow the shortest ground path connecting input vertices.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-densify

## Geometry · densify(method, distance, {deviation})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-55

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-densify

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　distance

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- distance
- The maximum distance between vertices. The actual distance between vertices will usually be less than the maximum distance, as new vertices will be evenly distributed along the original segment. If using a type of DISTANCE or ANGLE, the distance is measured in the units of the geometry's spatial reference. If using a type of GEODESIC, the distance is measured in meters.
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-densify

## Geometry · densify(method, distance, {deviation})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-56

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-densify

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　deviation

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- deviation （可選）
- Densify uses straight lines to approximate curves. You use deviation to control the accuracy of this approximation. The deviation is the maximum distance between the new segment and the original curve. The smaller its value, the more segments will be required to approximate the curve. If using a type of DISTANCE, the deviation is measured in the units of the geometry's spatial reference. If using a type of ANGLE, the deviation is measured in radians. If using a type of GEODESIC, the deviation is not used.
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-densify

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-getArea

## Geometry · getArea({method}, {units})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-74

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-getArea

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method （可選）
- The method used to measure area. GEODESIC—The shortest line between any two points on the earth's surface on a spheroid (ellipsoid). One use for a geodesic line is to determine the shortest distance between two cities for an airplane's flight path. This is also known as a great circle line if based on a sphere rather than an ellipsoid. GREAT_ELLIPTIC—The line on a spheroid (ellipsoid) defined by the intersection at the surface by a plane that passes through the center of the spheroid and the start and endpoints of a segment. This is also known as a great circle when a sphere is used. LOXODROME—The line follows a single compass bearing, or azimuth. Great circle routes are often broken into a series of loxodromes, which simplifies navigation. This is also known as a rhumb line. PLANAR—Planar measurements use 2D Cartesian mathematics to calculate lengths and areas. This option is only available when measuring in a projected coordinate system and the 2D plane of that coordinate system will be used as the basis for the measurements. Planar measurements reflect the projection of geographic data onto a 2D surface, and do not account for the curvature of the earth. PRESERVE_SHAPE—This type calculates the area or length of the geometry on the surface of the earth ellipsoid, for geometry defined in a projected or geographic coordinate system. This option preserves the shape of the geometry in its coordinate system. (默認值為 GEODESIC)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-getArea

**text**　如果指定了索引，則將返回幾何特定部分的 Point 對象的 Array 對象。 如果未指定索引，則返回的 Array 對象將包含每個幾何部分的 Point 對象的 Array 。
getPart 方法等同于建立對象索引；即 obj.getPart(0) 等同于 obj[0] 。

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-getPart

## Geometry · intersect(other, dimension)

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-84

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-intersect

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　構造一個幾何，它是兩個輸入幾何的幾何交集。 不同的維數可用于創建不同的 shape 類型。
對于同一 shape 類型的兩個幾何體，其交集為僅包含原始幾何重疊區域的幾何。
為了更快地獲取結果，請在調用 intersect 類之前先測試兩個幾何體是否 disjoint 。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-intersect

## Geometry · pointFromAngleAndDistance(angle, distance, {method})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-103

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-pointFromAngleAndDistance

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method （可選）
- Specifies the method that will be used to measure distance. GEODESIC—The shortest line between any two points on the earth's surface on a spheroid (ellipsoid). One use for a geodesic line is to determine the shortest distance between two cities for an airplane's flight path. This is also known as a great circle line if based on a sphere rather than an ellipsoid. GREAT_ELLIPTIC—The line on a spheroid (ellipsoid) defined by the intersection at the surface by a plane that passes through the center of the spheroid and the start and endpoints of a segment. This is also known as a great circle when a sphere is used. LOXODROME—The line follows a single compass bearing, or azimuth. Great circle routes are often broken into a series of loxodromes, which simplifies navigation. This is also known as a rhumb line. PLANAR—Planar measurements use 2D Cartesian mathematics to calculate lengths and areas. This option is only available when measuring in a projected coordinate system and the 2D plane of that coordinate system will be used as the basis for the measurements. Planar measurements reflect the projection of geographic data onto a 2D surface, and do not account for the curvature of the earth. (默認值為 GEODESIC)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-pointFromAngleAndDistance

## Geometry · positionAlongLine(value, {use_percentage}, {geodesic})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-106

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-positionAlongLine

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**parameter**　value

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- value
- The distance along the line. The distance will be evaluated based on the geodesic parameter value. If the geodesic parameter value is False, the distance will be evaluated in meters. If the geodesic parameter value is True, the distance will be evaluated in the units of the feature's spatial reference. If the value exceeds the length of the line or precedes the length of the line (a negative value), the behaviour will depend on the geodesic parameter value as follows: If the geodesic parameter value is False, exceeding the length of the line will return the endpoint of the line, and a negative distance value will return the starting point of the line. If the geodesic parameter value is True, exceeding or preceding the length of the line will cause the method to fail.
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-positionAlongLine

## Geometry · projectAs(spatial_reference, {transformation_name})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-110

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-projectAs

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　將幾何從一個空間參考投影到另一個空間參考。
當幾何對象和目標空間參考沒有公共基準面時，請指定 transformation_name 參數值。 有關詳細信息，請參閱 地理基準面變換 和 ListTransformations 。
當滿足以下條件時，將執行垂直變換：
幾何對象具有 z 座標。
幾何對象的空間參考和 spatial_reference 參數值均具有垂直座標系。
transformation_name 參數值為垂直變換。
當 transformation_name 參數值是垂直變換，并且幾何對象和目標空間參考都沒有垂直座標系時，函數將運算失敗，并顯示 ValueError 異常。
如果幾何對象或 spatial_reference 參數值具有 未知空間參考 ，則輸出幾何的空間參考將是 spatial_reference 參數值的空間參考。 不建議使用未知空間參考進行分析。
projectAs 方法不會修改 m 值（如果存在）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-projectAs

## Geometry · rotate({origin}, {rotation_angle})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-118

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-rotate

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　將幾何圍繞指定原點旋轉指定角度（以弧度表示）。
旋轉相對于原點應用，以便幾何圍繞原點旋轉。 原點的選擇對于旋轉幾何的位置影響很大。
旋轉不適用于 z 方向。
以下將介紹原點及其對輸出的影響的一些可能的選擇：
原點位于幾何外（不在幾何內或接觸幾何）- 幾何的位置將發生變化，但該幾何與其他以相同方式旋轉的幾何的相對位置保持不變。 位置將圍繞原點旋轉。
原點位于幾何的質心（質心位于幾何內部或接觸幾何）- 幾何將圍繞其質心旋轉。
原點位于幾何的頂點 - 幾何將圍繞所選頂點旋轉。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-rotate

- Point

## Geometry · scale({origin}, {sx}, {sy}, {sz})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-122

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-scale

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　從指定的原點沿 x、y 和 z 軸按指定的因子縮放幾何以創建新幾何。 已變換點（或頂點）的位置由以下提供：
\(( \space (x \space - \space origin_x) \space * \space scale_x \space + \space origin_x, \space (y \space - \space origin_y) \space * \space scale_y \space + \space origin_y, \space (z \space - \space origin_z) \space * \space scale_z \space + \space origin_z \space )\)
相對于原點應用變換，以使原點保持靜止，而幾何圍繞其擴展或收縮。 所生成幾何的擴展或收縮位置和方向受到原點的顯著影響。
以下將介紹原點及其對輸出的影響的一些可能的選擇：
原點位于幾何外（不在幾何內或接觸幾何） - 幾何的大小和位置將發生變化，但幾何將保持與以相同方式縮放的其他幾何的相對位置。 位置將遠離原點擴展或向原點收縮。
原點位于幾何的質心（如果質心位于幾何內部或接觸幾何）- 幾何的大小將發生變化，但幾何的位置將保持錨定在質心。 幾何將圍繞質心增大或收縮。
原點位于幾何的頂點 - 幾何的大小將發生變化，但幾何的位置將保持錨定在所選頂點。 幾何將從所選頂點開始增長或向頂點收縮。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-scale

**text**　構造一個幾何，該幾何是輸入幾何的并集。
兩個幾何必須具有同一形狀類型。
結果是單個幾何，它是兩個輸入幾何的組合。 任一輸入幾何中的任何內容都將成為結果的一部分。 但是，兩個幾何共有的內容在結果中只出現一次。
輸入幾何的順序對輸出沒有影響。

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-union

## Geometry · within(second_geometry, {relation})

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-145

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-within

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　指定基礎幾何是否位于比較幾何之內。
within 方法是與 contains 方法相反的運算符。
本圖僅顯示 True 關系。
如果基礎幾何是這些幾何的交集且其內部交集不為空，則基礎幾何位于比較幾何之內。 within 方法是 Clementini 運算符，在空的基礎幾何中除外。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-within

## Geometry · 代碼示例

**id**　55f665dc87d08fcb9f-150

**english**　Geometry

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-return-value-within

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/geometry.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Run the Copy Features tool, setting the output to the geometry object.
# geometries is returned as a list of geometry objects.
geometries = arcpy.management.CopyFeatures("c:/data/streets.shp",
                                           arcpy.Geometry())

# Walk through each geometry, totaling the length
length = 0
for geometry in geometries:
    length += geometry.length

print("Total length: {0}".format(length))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/geometry.html#method-return-value-within

## 創建資料列表 · 創建資料列表

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-11

**english**　Create lists of data

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**text**　每個函數的結果都是一個列表，即值的列表。 列表可以包含任何資料類型（例如字符串），它可以是指向資料集、欄位或表中行的路徑。 創建具有所需值的列表后，可在腳本中遍歷該列表以處理各個值。
了解有關列出工具、工具箱及環境設置的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

## 創建資料列表 · 列表函數參數

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-12

**english**　Create lists of data

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#42D

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**text**　這些函數的參數是相似的。 一些函數，例如 ListFields，需要輸入資料集值，因為函數列出的項目駐留在特定的對象或資料集中。 其他函數則不需要輸入資料集值，因為它們在當前工作空間中列出資料，該工作空間是在環境設置中定義的。 所有函數都有一個通配符參數，用于限制按名稱列出的對象或資料集。 通配符可定義一個名稱過濾器，新創建的列表中的所有內容都必須通過該過濾器。 例如，您可能想要列出工作空間中所有以字母 G 開頭的圖徵類。 下例說明了如何進行此操作：

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#42D

**text**　import arcpy
# Set the workspace. List all of the feature classes that start with 'G'
arcpy.env.workspace = "D:/St_Johns/data.gdb"
fcs = arcpy.ListFeatureClasses("G*")

**text**　ArcPy 使用 Python 列表類型作為其全部列表函數結果的返回類型，因為列表支持簡單資料訪問所需的靈活性和多種資料類型。 for 循環非常適用于處理列表，因為使用它可以一次一個項目的方式瀏覽列表。 for 循環可遍歷表中的每一個項目。 下面的示例是使用 for 循環遍歷前一個示例中生成的列表：

## 創建資料列表 · 搜索目錄和子目錄

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-26

**english**　Create lists of data

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#84C

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**text**　ArcPy 列表函數可用于遍歷單個目錄或工作空間，但在某些情況下，需要遍歷其他子文件夾和工作空間。 對于文件，通過使用 Python 的 os.walk 函數可以實現這一點，該函數用于遞歸迭代或遍歷文件夾，以找到其他子文件夾和文件。 但是，os.walk 嚴格基于文件并且無法識別資料庫和不基于文件的資料類型，而這些內容對 ArcGIS 很重要。 例如，os.walk 將無法看到文件地理資料庫工作空間或圖徵資料集中的網格資料集或其他內容。
在 arcpy.da 模塊中，Walk 函數也可用于遍歷目錄，還可瀏覽資料庫并標識 ArcGIS 資料類型。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#84C

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#84C

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-28

**chapter**

**text**　與列表函數不同，Walk 并不使用工作空間環境來標識其開始工作空間。 相反，Walk 遍歷的第一個開始（或 top）工作空間在其第一個參數 top 中指定。
在以下示例中，Walk 函數用于遍歷目錄樹并標識其中包含的所有面圖徵類。
使用 Walk 函數為面圖徵類創建目錄。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#84C

**text**　import arcpy
import os
workspace = "c:/data"
feature_classes = []
for dirpath, dirnames, datatypes in arcpy.da.Walk(workspace,
                                               datatype="FeatureClass",
                                               type="Polygon"):
 # Append all Polygon feature classes to a list for further processing
 for datatype in datatypes:
     feature_classes.append(os.path.join(dirpath, filename))

**text**　在某些情況下，可能存在在遍歷目錄樹時應避免的子目錄，例如，備份文件的目錄。 如果 topdown 參數為 True 或未指定，則可以就地修改工作空間，從而避開不需要的工作空間或在創建時添加其他工作空間。
使用 Walk 函數為網格資料創建目錄。 將忽略名為 back_up 的文件夾中的所有網格。

## 創建資料列表 · 注:

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-31

**english**　Create lists of data

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#84C

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**text**　import arcpy
import os
workspace = "c:/data"
rasters = []
for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in arcpy.da.Walk(
     workspace, topdown=True, datatype="RasterDataset"):
 # Disregard any folder named 'back_up' in creating list
 #  of rasters
 if "back_up" in dirnames:
     dirnames.remove('back_up')
 for filename in filenames:
     rasters.append(os.path.join(dirpath, filename))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#84C

## 創建資料列表 · 示例：使用 arcpy.da.Walk 分析資料

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-32

**english**　Create lists of data

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#688

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**text**　Walk 函數（以及 list 函數）通常用于批量處理資料。 以下腳本利用 arcpy.da.Walk 函數來分析企業級工作空間中的所有資料集。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#688

## 創建資料列表 · 示例：使用 arcpy.da.Walk 分析資料

**id**　5612e6e055443a085b-33

**english**　Create lists of data

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#688

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html

**text**　import arcpy
import os
# Enterprise workspace to be used
admin_workspace = "Database Connections/tenone@sde.sde"
analyze_contents = []
for dirpath, workspaces, datatypes in arcpy.da.Walk(
     admin_workspace,
     followlinks=True,
     datatype=['Table', 'FeatureClass', 'RasterDataset']):
 # Create full path, and add tables, feature classes, raster datasets
 analyze_contents += [
     os.path.join(dirpath, datatype) for datatype in datatypes]
 # create full path, add the feature datasets of the .sde file
 analyze_contents += [
     os.path.join(dirpath, workspace) for workspace in workspaces]
# Run Analyze Datasets on the complete list
arcpy.management.AnalyzeDatasets(admin_workspace,
                              "SYSTEM",
                              analyze_contents,
                              "ANALYZE_BASE",
                              "ANALYZE_DELTA",
                              "ANALYZE_ARCHIVE")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/listing-data.html#688

**text**　BIM 文件工作空間屬性示例

## RasterToXarray · 討論

**id**　56927d6dd45776e9bd-2

**english**　RasterToXarray

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 轉換

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/rastertoxarray.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/rastertoxarray.html

**text**　Xarray 是一個 Python 模塊，支持帶有維度、座標和屬性標注的多維數組。它基于 NumPy 和 pandas 構建并與之整合，可處理 Python SciPy 生態系統中的多維資料以用于數值計算。有關詳細信息，請參閱 xarray 文檔 。
您可能需要將 ArcGIS 多維網格轉換為 xarray 資料集才能執行以下類型的運算：
在 xarray 模塊中實施多種函數之一，例如，對 xarray.DataArray 對象運行多維插值、重采樣和群運算。
利用其他 Python 模塊，它們直接使用多種屬性域中的 xarray.DataArray 對象，包括地球科學、機器學習等。例如，導入 EOF（經驗正交函數）Python 包并對 xarray.DataArray 中的資料執行 EOF 分析。
將多維網格轉換為 xarray.DataArray ，然后轉換為其他 Python 資料類型或格式，例如 pandas DataFrame 對象或 netCDF。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/rastertoxarray.html#discussion

## RasterToXarray · 代碼示例

**id**　56927d6dd45776e9bd-7

**english**　RasterToXarray

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 轉換

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/rastertoxarray.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/rastertoxarray.html

**text**　import arcpy
import xarray

# Get input Raster multidimensional information
inRas = arcpy.Raster('C:/data/temperature_3hour.crf', True)
inRas.mdinfo

# Convert Raster to Xarray Dataset
xrr = arcpy.ia.RasterToXarray(inRas)

# Upsample the data from originally 3 hour to hourly time interval
newXrr = xrr.resample(StdTime='1H').nearest(tolerance="1H")

#Convert Xarray dataset back to raster
newRaster = arcpy.ia.XarrayToRaster(newXrr)
newRaster.save("C:/output/temperature_hourly.crf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/rastertoxarray.html#code-sample

**text**　用于將趨勢網格轉換為三波段 RGB（紅、綠和藍）網格。
將根據 GenerateTrend 函數生成趨勢網格。 TrendToRGB 函數也可以用于由 使用 CCDC 分析變化 工具生成的變化分析網格。

## TrendToRGB · 討論

**id**　572b0ae8a707d1321f-2

**english**　TrendToRGB

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 轉換

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/trendtorgb.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/trendtorgb.html

**text**　系統提供兩個模型轉換選項：
線性 - 來自模型的線性趨勢信息將轉換為 RGB。可以使用此選項來可視化線性趨勢模型的斜率和截距系數。
諧波 - 來自模型的諧波趨勢信息將轉換為 RGB。可以使用此選項來可視化趨勢網格或變化分析網格中的諧波回歸系數。
有關此函數工作原理的詳細信息，請參閱 趨勢轉 RGB 網格函數。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要將其設置為永久，可以調用網格對象的 save 方法。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/trendtorgb.html#discussion

## TrendToRGB · 代碼示例

**id**　572b0ae8a707d1321f-8

**english**　TrendToRGB

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 轉換

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/trendtorgb.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/trendtorgb.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set the analysis environments
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/arcpyExamples/data"

# Set the local variables
trend_raster = "precip_trend.tif"
model_type = "HARMONIC"

# convert the trend raster to a RGB raster
precipTrend_rgb = TrendToRGB(trend_raster,model_type)

# save the output
precipTrend_rgb.save("C:/arcpyExamples/outputs/precipTrend_rgb.tif")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/trendtorgb.html#code-sample

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html

**text**　FieldMap 對象的屬性包括輸入文本值的起始位置和結束位置，因此可以使用一組輸入值創建輸出值。 如果 FieldMap 對象包含多個來自同一表或圖徵類的輸入欄位，則將使用 mergeRule 屬性合并每個記錄的值。 這是一種連接值的簡便方法，例如在一個欄位中保存的街道名稱和在另一個欄位中保存的街道類型，如 Eureka 和 Street。 當指定 Join 作為 mergeRule 值時，會使用 FieldMap 對象的 joinDelimiter 屬性。 任何字符集（包括空格）都可以用作分隔符。 在上方示例中，將會創建值 Eureka Street。
FieldMappings 對象是一組 FieldMap 對象，用作欄位映射工具的參數值，例如 合并 。 要處理這些對象，最直接的方法就是先創建 FieldMappings 對象，然后通過添加要組合的輸入圖徵類或表對其 FieldMap 對象進行初始化。 提供了所有輸入后， FieldMappings 對象將為所有輸入中的每個唯一欄位名提供一個 FieldMap 對象或輸出欄位。 通過添加新欄位或移除不需要的輸出欄位來修改此列表。 要更改 FieldMap 對象的 outputField 屬性，請將此屬性分配給變量。 然后更改變量的屬性（如 name 和 type ），并將 outputField 屬性重新分配給此變量。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#discussion

## FieldMappings · loadFromString(string)

**id**　5776d12e3b32a4b7cd-22

**english**　FieldMappings

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 欄位

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-loadFromString

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html

**parameter**　string

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- string
- The string representation of the object. In addition to FieldMappings and FieldMap methods and properties, you can also construct a FieldMappings object from a formatted string. The following example shows the creation of a FieldMappings object that could be used with the Merge tool. import arcpy fieldmappings_string = 'REGION "Region" true true false 21 Text -1 -1,First,#,c:\\data\\fgdb.gdb\\north_america,REGION,-1,-1,c:\\data\\fgdb.gdb\\south_america,REGION2,-1,-1' fieldmappings = arcpy.FieldMappings() fieldmappings.loadFromString(fieldmappings_string) The first nine values in the string define an output field and are space delimited. The name of the output field. The alias of the output field. Whether the output field is editable (true or false). Whether the output field supports nulls (true or false). Whether the output field is required (true or false). The length of the output field (text fields only). The field type of the output field. The precision of the output field (float and double fields only). The scale of the output field (float and double fields only). The remaining values define the field map characteristics and are comma delimited. The field map merge rule. The concatenator to join values. The path to the input table. The field name from the input table. The start position of an input text value. The end position of an input text value. Any number of input fields can be mapped to the output field, not only two, as implied in the example. Include the merge rule and concatenator once, and include the dataset path, field name, and start position and end position for each input field. Enclose any values with spaces, such as the field alias or concatenator, in quotation marks. To skip a value, use a # for string values, and -1 for numeric values. As shown in the following example, use a semicolon delimiter to separate multiple output fields. import arcpy fieldmappings_string = 'REGION "Region" true true false 21 Text -1 -1,First,#,c:\\data\\fgdb.gdb\\north_america,REGION,-1,-1,c:\\data\\fgdb.gdb\\south_america,REGION2,-1,-1;CAPITAL "Capital" true true false 21 Text -1 -1,First,#,c:\\data\\fgdb.gdb\\north_america,CAPITAL,-1,-1,c:\\data\\fgdb.gdb\\south_america,CAPITAL2,-1,-1' fieldmappings = arcpy.FieldMappings() fieldmappings.loadFromString(fieldmappings_string)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-loadFromString

## FieldMappings · 代碼示例

**id**　5776d12e3b32a4b7cd-29

**english**　FieldMappings

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 欄位

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html

**text**　要將相似資料集合并到一個無所不包的資料集中時，通常使用 FieldMap 對象。 在本例中，Trees 圖徵類和 Plants.shp shapefile 會合并至一個圖徵類：Vegetation。 兩個原始圖徵類都具有兩個屬性： Type 和 Diameter 。 兩個屬性都必須通過合并進行維護。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

## FieldMappings · 代碼示例

**id**　5776d12e3b32a4b7cd-30

**english**　FieldMappings

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 欄位

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Set the workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = 'c:/base'

in_features_1 = 'data.gdb/Trees'
in_features_2 = 'Plants.shp'
out_features = 'data.gdb/Vegetation'

# Create the required FieldMap and FieldMappings objects.
fm_type = arcpy.FieldMap()
fm_diam = arcpy.FieldMap()
fms = arcpy.FieldMappings()

# Get the field names of vegetation type and diameter for both
# original files.
tree_type = "Tree_Type"
plant_type = "Plant_Type"

tree_diam = "Tree_Diameter"
plant_diam = "Diameter"

# Add fields to their corresponding FieldMap objects.
fm_type.addInputField(in_features_1, tree_type)
fm_type.addInputField(in_features_2, plant_type)

fm_diam.addInputField(in_features_1, tree_diam)
fm_diam.addInputField(in_features_2, plant_diam)

# Set the output field properties for both FieldMap objects by creating a new variable.
type_field = fm_type.outputField
type_field.name = 'Veg_Type'
# Set the new variable back into the field map's outputField.
fm_type.outputField = type_field

diam_field = fm_diam.outputField
diam_field.name = 'Veg_Diam'
fm_diam.outputField = diam_field

# Add the FieldMap objects to the FieldMappings object.
fms.addFieldMap(fm_type)
fms.addFieldMap(fm_diam)

# Merge the two feature classes.
arcpy.management.Merge([in_features_1, in_features_2], out_features, fms)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

## FieldMappings · 代碼示例

**id**　5776d12e3b32a4b7cd-31

**english**　FieldMappings

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 欄位

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html

**text**　此示例演示了通過 ExportFeatures 函數使用 FieldMap 對象合并欄位的操作。 在本示例中，圖徵類包含有關城市中每個十字路口的事故數量的信息。 每年的資料都保存在一個欄位中。 用戶希望在不更改現有表格的情況下找出每個十字路口的平均事故數量。

**content_version**　3.7

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

## FieldMappings · 代碼示例

**id**　5776d12e3b32a4b7cd-32

**english**　FieldMappings

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 欄位

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html

**text**　import arcpy

in_features = r'c:/base/data.gdb/AccidentData'
out_features = r'c:/base/data.gdb/AverageAccidents'

# Create the necessary FieldMap and FieldMappings objects.
fm = arcpy.FieldMap()
fm1 = arcpy.FieldMap()
fms = arcpy.FieldMappings()

# Each field with accident data begins with 'Yr'.
# The next step loops through each of the fields beginning with 'Yr',
# and adds them to the FieldMap object.
for field in arcpy.ListFields(in_features, 'Yr*'):
    fm.addInputField(in_features, field.name)

# Set the merge rule to find the mean value of all fields in the
# FieldMap object.
fm.mergeRule = 'Mean'

# Set the properties of the output field by creating a new variable.
out_field = fm.outputField
out_field.name = 'AvgAccidents'
out_field.aliasName = 'AvgAccidents'
# Set the new variable back into the field map's outputField
fm.outputField = out_field

# Add the intersection field to the second FieldMap object.
fm1.addInputField(in_features, "Intersection")

# Add both FieldMap objects to the FieldMappings object.
fms.addFieldMap(fm)
fms.addFieldMap(fm1)

# Create the output feature class using the FieldMappings object.
arcpy.conversion.ExportFeatures(in_features, out_features, field_mapping=fms)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/fieldmappings.html#method-replaceFieldMap

## Apply · 語法

**id**　57b0b328fa456923f7-4

**english**　Apply

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- in_raster
- The input raster dataset or datasets. The input can include the following: A single raster—For raster functions that require one raster input, and the raster function argument name for the raster input is Raster. For example, to apply the NDVI raster function to a raster object, the input would be raster_object. A collection of rasters in a tuple—For raster functions that require two or more raster inputs, and the raster function argument names for the raster inputs are Raster, Raster2, Raster3, etc. For example, to apply the Arithmetic raster function to perform a mathematical operation on two rasters, the input would be (raster_object_1,raster_object_2). An array of rasters in a list—For raster functions that require an array of raster inputs, and the raster function argument name for the inputs is Rasters. For example, to apply the Composite Bands raster function to combine three rasters into a single raster, the input would be [raster_object_1,raster_object_2,raster_object_3]. A dictionary with names and rasters—For raster functions that require a set of names raster inputs. For example, to apply the Heat Index raster function to calculate apparent temperature, the input would be ['temperature': raster_object_1,'rh': raster_object_2].
- Raster

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html#syntax

**values**　
- raster_function_arguments
- The parameters and values associated with the raster function or function chain. If not specified, and if applicable, default values will be used. For example, the Tasseled Cap raster function does not require any arguments; the input is a single raster, so there is no need to specify arguments. The Arithmetic raster function, however, requires 5 input parameters: Raster, Raster2, Operation, Cellsize Type and Extent Type. To use Arithmetic with the Apply function, Raster and Raster2 are defined in the in_raster parameter, and the remaining parameters have default values, so if nothing is specified for those parameters, the default values will be used. For information about the function arguments for each raster function, see Raster function objects.
- Dictionary

## Apply · 代碼示例

**id**　57b0b328fa456923f7-9

**english**　Apply

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html

**text**　# Apply NDVI, Arithmetic, and Composite Bands functions to one or more raster datasets.

import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set input raster for NDVI
Landsat = Raster("Landsat8_1_12_18.tif")

# Apply NDVI raster function to a single raster
out_NDVI_raster = Apply(Landsat, "NDVI", {'VisibleBandID':4,'InfraredBandID':5})

# Set input rasters for Arithmetic
DEM = Raster("DEM.tif")
Buildings = Raster("BuildingHeights.tif")

# Apply Arithmetic raster function to add building height to ground elevation
out_Arithmetic_raster = Apply((DEM, Buildings),'Plus')

# Set input rasters for Composite Bands
Band1 = Raster("L8_MSB1.tif")
Band2 = Raster("L8_MSB2.tif")
Band3 = Raster("L8_MSB3.tif")

# Apply Composite Bands function to combine three bands into a single raster
out_composite_rasters = Apply([Band1, Band2, Band3], 'CompositeBand')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html#code-sample

## Apply · 代碼示例

**id**　57b0b328fa456923f7-11

**english**　Apply

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/apply.html

**text**　# Apply Heat Index function to multidimensional raster objects

import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set input multidimensional raster dataset
in_raster = Raster(r"C:\test.gdb\ClimateData", True)

# Select the relative humidity variable
RH = Subset(ClimateData, variables = 'rh')

# Select the temperature variable
Temp = Subset(ClimateData, variables = 't')

# Apply the Heat Index function to the two multidimensional raster subsets
# The output is a multidimensional raster where each slice is the heat index for the slice's time
heat_index_raster = Apply({'temperature': Temp, 'rh': RH}, 'HeatIndex', {'units':'Kelvin', 'outUnits':'Fahrenheit'})

## LessThanFrequency · 語法

**id**　581e584876e4d61d50-6

**english**　LessThanFrequency

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- extent_type
- The method that will be used to compute the extent of the output when the input rasters have different extents. FirstOf—The output extent will be defined using the extent of the first input raster. LastOf—The output extent will be defined using the extent of the last input raster. IntersectionOf—The output extent will be defined as the intersecting area of the input rasters. UnionOf—The output extent will be defined as the total extent of the input rasters. (默認值為 FirstOf)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html#syntax

## LessThanFrequency · 語法

**id**　581e584876e4d61d50-7

**english**　LessThanFrequency

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- cellsize_type
- The method that will be used to compute the pixel size of the output when the input rasters have different pixel sizes. FirstOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the pixel size of the first input raster. LastOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the pixel size of the last input raster. MaxOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the maximum pixel size of the input rasters. MeanOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the mean of both input rasters. MinOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the minimum pixel size of the input rasters. (默認值為 FirstOf)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html#syntax

## LessThanFrequency · 語法

**id**　581e584876e4d61d50-8

**english**　LessThanFrequency

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- process_as_multiband
- Specifies how the bands of the input rasters will be processed. True—Each multiband raster will be processed as a multiband raster. The operation will be performed for each band from one input using the corresponding band number from other inputs. False—Each band from a multiband raster input will be processed separately as a single-band raster. (默認值為 False)
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/lessthanfrequency.html#syntax

## LessThanFrequency · 代碼示例

**id**　581e584876e4d61d50-11

**english**　LessThanFrequency

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

#Set the local variables
value_raster = r"c:\temp\value_raster.tif"

rasters = [r"c:\temp\raster1.tif", r"c:\temp\raster2.tif", r"c:\temp\raster3.tif"]

# Run
LessThan_raster = LessThanFrequency(value_raster, rasters, "UnionOf", "FirstOf", True)

## 層屬性 · 代碼示例

**id**　5823220cf19348fee3-26

**english**　Tier properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 公共設施網絡屬性
- 域網絡屬性
- 層

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/tiers.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/tiers.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name:        DescribeUN_TierProperties.py
Description: This script reports the tier properties of a utility network
Created by:  Esri
****************************************************************************'''

# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Describe functions on a Utility Network
UN = "C:\\Projects\\MyProject\\unowner.sde\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville"
d = arcpy.Describe(UN)

# Domain Network properties
domnets = d.domainNetworks
for dom in domnets:
    print("*** - Domain Network properties - ***")
    print(f"Domain Network Creation Time: {dom.creationTime}")
    print(f"Domain Network Release Number: {dom.releaseNumber}")
    print(f"Domain Network Name: {dom.domainNetworkName}")
    print(f"Domain Network ID: {dom.domainNetworkId}")
    print(f"Domain Network Alias Name: {dom.domainNetworkAliasName}")
    print(f"Domain Network is Structure Network: {dom.isStructureNetwork}")
    print(f"Domain Network Tier Definition: {dom.tierDefinition}")
    print(f"Domain Network Subnetwork Controller Type: {dom.subnetworkControllerType} \n")
    print(f"Domain Network Subnetwork Table Name: {dom.subnetworkTableName}")
    print(f"Domain Network Subnetwork Label Field Name: {dom.subnetworkLabelFieldName}")

    for tier in dom.tiers:
        print(f"Tier Name: {tier.name}")
        print(f"Rank: {tier.rank}")
        print(f"Tier topology type: {tier.tierTopology}")
        print(f"Tier group name: {tier.tierGroupName} \n")
        print(f"Subnetwork field name: {tier.subnetworkFieldName}")
        print(f"Supports disjoint subnetwork: {tier.supportDisjointSubnetwork}")
        print(f"Update Subnetwork Edit Mode For Default Version: {tier.updateSubnetworkEditModeForDefaultVersion}")
        print(f"Update Subnetwork Edit Mode For Named Version: {tier.updateSubnetworkEditModeForNamedVersion}")
        print(f"Update Subnetwork On Structures: {tier.updateSubnetworkOnStructures}")
        print(f"Update Subnetwork On Containers: {tier.updateSubnetworkOnContainers}")
        print(f"Diagram templates: {tier.diagramTemplates} \n")
        print(f"Manage IsDirty: {tier.ManageSubnetwork.isDirty} \n")

        # Subnetwork Controller Properties
        for sc in tier.validSubnetworkControllers:
            print(" -- Subnetwork Controllers Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {sc.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in sc.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode} \n")

        # Valid Device Properties
        for vd in tier.validDevices:
            print(" -- Valid Devices Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {vd.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in vd.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode} \n")

        # Valid Lines Properties
        for vl in tier.validLines:
            print(" -- Valid Lines Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {vl.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in vl.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode}")

        # Valid Junctions Properties
        for vj in tier.validJunctions:
            print(" -- Valid Junctions Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {vj.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in vj.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode}")

        # Valid Junction Objects Properties
        for vjo in tier.validJunctionObjects:
            print(" -- Valid JunctionObjects Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {vjo.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in vjo.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode}")

        # Valid Junction Objects Subnetwork Controller Properties
        for vjosc in tier.validJunctionObjectSubnetworkControllers:
            print(" -- Valid Junction Object Subnetwork Controllers Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {vjosc.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in vjosc.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode} \n")

        # Valid Edge Objects Properties
        for veo in tier.validEdgeObjects:
            print(" -- Valid EdgeObjects Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {veo.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in veo.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode}")

        # Aggregated Lines for SubnetLine Properties
        for al in tier.aggregatedLinesForSubnetLine:
            print(" -- Aggregated Lines for SubnetLine Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {al.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in al.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode} \n")

        # Update Subnetwork Trace Configuration Properties
        print(" -- Update Subnetwork Trace Properties -- ")
        ust = tier.updateSubnetworkTraceConfiguration
        print(f"Include Containers: {ust.includeContainers}")
        print(f"Include Content: {ust.includeContent}")
        print(f"Include Structures: {ust.includeStructures}")
        print(f"Include Barriers: {ust.includeBarriers}")
        print(f"Validate Consistency: {ust.validateConsistency}")
        print(f"Validate Locatability: {ust.validateLocatability}")
        print(f"Include Isolated: {ust.includeIsolated}")
        print(f"Ignore Barriers at Starting Points: {ust.ignoreBarriersAtStartingPoints}")
        print(f"Include Up To First Spatial Container: {ust.includeUpToFirstSpatialContainer}")
        print(f"Allow Indeterminate Flow: {ust.allowIndeterminateFlow}")
        print(f"Domain Network Name: {ust.domainNetworkName}")
        print(f"Tier Name: {ust.tierName}")
        print(f"Target Tier Name: {ust.targetTierName}")
        print(f"Subnetwork Name: {ust.subnetworkName}")
        print(f"Diagram Template Name: {ust.diagramTemplateName}")
        print(f"Shortest Path Network Attribute Name: {ust.shortestPathNetworkAttributeName}")
        print(f"Filter Bitset Network Attribute Name: {ust.filterBitsetNetworkAttributeName}")
        print(f"Traversability Scope: {ust.traversabilityScope}")
        print(f"Filter Scope: {ust.filterScope} \n")

        # Condition Barrier Properties
        print(" - Condition Barrier Properties - ")
        for cb in ust.conditionBarriers:
            try:
                print(f"Name: {cb.name} ")
                print(f"Type: {cb.type} ")
                print(f"Operator: {cb.operator} ")
                print(f"Value: {cb.value} ")
                print(f"CombineUsingOr: {cb.combineUsingOr}")
                print(f"Is Specific Value: {cb.isSpecificValue} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped condition barrier properties. \n")

        # Function Barrier Properties
        print(" - Function Barrier Properties - ")
        for fb in ust.functionBarriers:
            try:
                print(f"Name: {fb.networkAttributeName}")
                print(f"Type: {fb.functionType}")
                print(f"Operator: {fb.networkAttributeOperator}")
                print(f"Value: {fb.value} ")
                print(f"Use Local Values: {fb.useLocalValues} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped function barrier properties. \n")

        # Filter Barrier Properties
        print(" - Filter Barrier Properties - ")
        for filb in ust.filterBarriers:
            try:
                print(f"Name: {filb.name}")
                print(f"Type: {filb.type}")
                print(f"Operator: {filb.operator}")
                print(f"Value: {filb.value}")
                print(f"CombineUsingOr: {filb.combineUsingOr}")
                print(f"Is Specific Value: {filb.isSpecificValue} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped filter barrier properties. \n")

        # Filter Function Barrier Properties
        print(" - Filter Function Barrier Properties - ")
        for ffb in ust.filterFunctionBarriers:
            try:
                print(f"Name: {ffb.networkAttributeName}")
                print(f"Type: {ffb.functionType}")
                print(f"Operator: {ffb.networkAttributeOperator}")
                print(f"Value: {ffb.value}")
                print(f"Use Local Values: {ffb.useLocalValues} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped filter function properties. \n")

        # Functions Properties
        print(" - Functions Properties - ")
        for f in ust.functions:
            # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
            try:
                print(f"Function Type: {f.functionType}")
                print(f"Function Network Attribute Name: {f.networkAttributeName}")
                print(f"Function Summary Attribute Name: {f.summaryAttributeName} \n")
                # Function Conditions
                print(" - Function Conditions - ")
                for fc in f.conditions:
                    print(f"Name: {fc.name}")
                    print(f"Type: {fc.type}")
                    print(f"Operator: {fc.operator}")
                    print(f"Value: {fc.value}")
                    print(f"CombineUsingOr: {fc.combineUsingOr}")
                    print(f"Is Specific Value: {fc.isSpecificValue} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped functions properties. \n")

        # Nearest Neighbor Properties
        print(" - Nearest Neighbor Properties - ")
        nn = ust.nearestNeighbor
        # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
        try:
            print(f"Count: {nn.count}")
            print(f"Cost Network Attribute Name: {nn.costNetworkAttributeName}")
            print(f"Nearest Categories: {nn.nearestCategories} \n")
            print(f" - Nearest Asset Properties - ")
            for nsta in nn.nearestAssets:
                try:
                    print(f"Network Source ID: {nsta.networkSourceID}")
                    print(f"Asset Group Code: {nsta.assetGroupCode}")
                    print(f"Asset Type Code: {nsta.assetTypeCode} \n")
                except:
                    print("Skipped nearest assets properties. \n")
        except:
            print("Skipped nearest neighbor properties. \n")

        # Output Filter Properties
        print(" - Output Filter Properties - ")
        for ofp in ust.outputFilters:
            # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
            try:
                for of in ofp:
                    print(f"Network Source ID: {of.networkSourceID}")
                    print(f"Asset Group Code: {of.assetGroupCode}")
                    print(f"Asset Type Code: {of.assetTypeCode} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped output filter properties. \n")

        # Output Condition Properties
        print(" - Output Condition Properties - ")
        for oc in ust.outputConditions:
            # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
            try:
                print(f"Name: {oc.name}")
                print(f"Type: {oc.type}")
                print(f"Operator: {oc.operator}")
                print(f"Value: {oc.value}")
                print(f"CombineUsingOr: {oc.combineUsingOr}")
                print(f"Is Specific Value: {oc.isSpecificValue} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped output condition properties. \n")

        # Propagators Properties
        print(" - Propagator Properties - ")
        for p in ust.propagators:
            # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
            try:
                print(f"Network Attribute Name: {p.networkAttributeName}")
                print(f"Trace Propagator Function Type: {p.tracePropagatorFunctionType}")
                print(f"Network Attribute Filter Operator: {p.networkAttributeOperator}")
                print(f"Network Attribute Value: {p.value}")
                print(f"Propagated Attribute Name: {p.propagatedAttributeName}")
                print(f"Substitution Attribute Name: {p.substitutionAttributeName}")
            except:
                print("Skipped propagator properties. \n")

## “深度學習”地理處理函數 · “深度學習”地理處理函數

**id**　5856eb80408ce7b732-1

**english**　Deep Learning geoprocessing functions

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 地理處理函數

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/deep-learning-geoprocessing-functions.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/deep-learning-geoprocessing-functions.html

**text**　需具有 Image Analyst 許可才能使用。
“深度學習”地理處理函數用于訓練深度學習模型、檢測圖像中的特定圖徵、對網格資料集中的像素進行分類。
深度學習是一種機器學習人工智能，它使用神經網絡中的多個圖層來檢測影像中的圖徵，其中每個圖層能夠提取影像中的一個或多個唯一圖徵。 深度學習類別中的地理處理函數利用 GPU 處理來及時執行分析。
這些 ArcPy 地理處理函數使用在第三方深度學習框架（例如 CNTK 和 PyTorch）中訓練的模型來檢測特定圖徵，并輸出圖徵或類地圖。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/deep-learning-geoprocessing-functions.html

## “深度學習”地理處理函數 · “深度學習”地理處理函數

**id**　5856eb80408ce7b732-2

**english**　Deep Learning geoprocessing functions

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 地理處理函數

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/deep-learning-geoprocessing-functions.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/deep-learning-geoprocessing-functions.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 地理處理函數
- 描述

**values**　
- 使用深度學習分類對象
- 用于運行輸入網格和可選圖徵類上的訓練深度學習模型，以生成圖徵類或表，其中每個輸入對象或圖徵均具有一個分配的類或類別標注。 該工具需要包含經過訓練的模型信息的模型定義文件。 可以使用訓練深度學習模型工具或第三方訓練軟件（例如 PyTorch）訓練模型。 模型定義文件可以是 Esri 模型定義 JSON 文件 (.emd) 或深度學習模型包，它必須包含為處理每個對象調用的 Python 網格函數的路徑以及經過訓練的二進制深度學習模型文件的路徑。

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/deep-learning-geoprocessing-functions.html

**text**　輸入網格必須為 3 波段無符號 8 位影像。
要使用此函數，指定 spectral_detail 和 spatial_detail 參數或 spectral_radius 和 spatial_radius 參數。這兩個參數對關系的相反。
有關此函數工作原理的詳細信息，請參閱 Mean Shift 影像分割 網格函數。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要將其設置為永久，可以調用網格對象的 save 方法。

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set the local variables
raster = "C:/data/Multispectral_Landsat.tif"
spectral_detail= 15.5
spatial_detail= 15
spectral_radius=None
spatial_radius=None
min_num_pixels_per_segment= 20

# Apply RegionGrow function
arcpy.ia.SegMeanShift(raster, spectral_detail, spatial_detail, spectral_radius, spatial_radius, min_num_pixels_per_segment)

# Save the output
segmented_raster.save("C:/arcpyExamples/outputs/Multispectral_Landsat_seg.crf")

## FuzzyGaussian · 討論

**id**　586520d734b13d4869-2

**english**　FuzzyGaussian

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 模糊隸屬度類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/fuzzygaussian-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/fuzzygaussian-class.html

**text**　使用 FuzzyGaussian 對象的工具： 模糊隸屬度 。
模糊高斯函數的方程為：
方程的輸入為 f1 （散度 (spread)）和 f2 （中點 (midpoint)）。增大散度會導致模糊隸屬度曲線變得陡峭。
高斯函數在特定值的附近進行分類時十分有用。
輸入值可以為整型值或浮點型正值。
高斯函數與近鄰 (Near) 函數類似，但高斯函數的階差 (spread) 更小。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/fuzzygaussian-class.html#discussion

## FuzzyGaussian · 代碼示例

**id**　586520d734b13d4869-11

**english**　FuzzyGaussian

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 模糊隸屬度類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/fuzzygaussian-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/fuzzygaussian-class.html

**text**　# Name: FuzzyGaussian_Ex_02.py
# Description: Scales input raster data into values ranging from zero to one
#     indicating the strength of a membership in a set.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "as_std"

# Create the FuzzyGaussian algorithm object
midpoint = 10
spread = 0.2
myFuzzyAlgorithm = FuzzyGaussian(midpoint, spread)

# Run FuzzyMembership
outFuzzyMember = FuzzyMembership(inRaster, myFuzzyAlgorithm)

# Save the output
outFuzzyMember.save("c:/sapyexamples/fzygauss")

**content_version**　3.7

**text**　通過基于維度間隔和聚合方法組合輸入多維網格中的剖切來創建網格對象。

**content_version**　3.7

## Aggregate · 討論

**id**　5897dd425216db0a85-2

**english**　Aggregate

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html

**text**　使用 Aggregate 函數可根據給定變量聚合多維網格中的變量值。 例如，如果您擁有每日降水量資料，則可以確定月平均降水量值。
此函數可創建一個網格對象，該網格對象為輸入多維網格的聚合。 默認情況下，系統將計算與給定維度關聯的所有變量的聚合。 例如，如果降水量、溫度和風速都具有時間維度，在將 dimension_name 設置為時間維度的情況下，系統將分別聚合所有三個變量。 如果您只想聚合一個變量，請使用 Subset 函數，然后使用 Aggregate 。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要使其變為永久性資料集，請調用網格對象的 save 方法。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#discussion

## Aggregate · 語法

**id**　5897dd425216db0a85-6

**english**　Aggregate

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- raster_function
- The name of a raster function as a string or the path to a custom raster function (.rft.xml file) that will be used to compute the aggregated pixel values. The following are the supported raster functions as strings: Majority—The pixel value that occurs most frequently will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. MajorityContinueOnNoData—The pixel value that occurs most frequently will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Max—The maximum value of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. MaxContinueOnNoData—The maximum value of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Mean—The mean of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. MeanContinueOnNoData—The mean of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Med—The median of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. MedContinueOnNoData—The median of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Min—The minimum of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. MinContinueOnNoData—The minimum of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Minority—The pixel value that occurs least frequently will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. MinorityContinueOnNoData—The pixel value that occurs least frequently will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Percentile—The percentile of values for a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. PercentileContinueOnNoData—The percentile of values for a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Range—The range of values for a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. RangeContinueOnNoData—The range of values for a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Std—The standard deviation of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. StdContinueOnNoData—The standard deviation of a pixel will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Sum—The sum of a pixel's values will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. SumContinueOnNoData—The sum of a pixel's values will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored. Variety—The number of unique pixel values will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData will be returned if there are any NoData pixels along the dimension. VarietyContinueOnNoData—The number of unique pixel values will be calculated across all slices in the interval, and NoData pixels will be ignored.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#syntax

## Aggregate · 語法

**id**　5897dd425216db0a85-8

**english**　Aggregate

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- aggregation_definition
- The dimension interval for which the data will be aggregated. The key-value pairs must follow one of the following formats: 'interval':<interval_keyword> Use the interval keyword only when dimension_name is a time dimension. Interval keywords include HOURLY, DAILY, WEEKLY, MONTHLY, QUARTERLY, and YEARLY. For example, use this option to aggregate daily temperature data into monthly temperature data for each year. The output will include no more than 12 slices for each year. 'recurrent_interval':<recurrent_interval_keyword> Use the recurrent interval keyword only when dimension_name is a time dimension. Recurrent interval keywords include DAILY, WEEKLY, MONTHLY, and QUARTERLY. For example, use this option to calculate the average temperature value for each month across all the years in the dataset. The output will include no more than 12 slices (one slice for each month of the year). 'interval_value':<value>, 'interval_unit':<unit> Use the interval value to define recurring intervals. The interval unit is required when dimension_name is a time dimension. Interval unit keywords include HOUR, DAY, WEEK, MONTH, QUARTER, and YEAR. For example, use this option to aggregate daily temperature into temperature every 10 days. 'interval_ranges':[(min, max),(min, max), ...] Use the interval ranges option to define custom intervals for aggregation. For example, use this option to aggregate daily temperature into temperature across specific seasonal dates. If not specified, all slices across the dimension will be aggregated. (默認值為 None)
- Dictionary

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#syntax

**text**　通過計算總體平均值、月平均值、10 天中的最小值以及 2 個范圍中的最大值來聚合降水量資料。

**content_version**　3.7

## Aggregate · 代碼示例

**id**　5897dd425216db0a85-12

**english**　Aggregate

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html

**text**　import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

in_raster = Raster('ClimateData_Daily.nc', True)

# Choose the precipitation variable
prcp_raster = Subset(in_raster, variables = 'prcp')

# Calculate the average precipitation across the whole time period
avg_prcp = Aggregate(prcp_raster, 'StdTime', 'Mean')

# Calculate the monthly mean precipitation
monthly_avg_prcp = Aggregate(prcp_raster, 'StdTime',
    'Mean', {'interval': 'monthly'})

# Calculate the minimum precipitation every 10 days
min_precip_10day = Aggregate(prcp_raster, 'StdTime', 'Min',
    {'interval_value' : 10, 'interval_unit': 'day'})

# Calculate the maximum precipitation in two separate time ranges
max_prcp_range = Aggregate(prcp_raster, 'StdTime', 'Max',
    {'interval_ranges':  [('2001-01-01T00:00:00', '2001-02-01T00:00:00'),
    ('2001-12-01T00:00:00', '2001-12-24T00:00:00')]})

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/aggregate.html#code-sample

## Edge Source Properties · 代碼示例

**id**　58d3204f04c4c8ff5d-11

**english**　Edge Source Properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 網絡資料集屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/edge-source.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/edge-source.html

**text**　# Name: NDSAttributeProperties_ex01.py
# Description: Print the information about edge sources defined for the
#              network dataset

import arcpy
import sys

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Data/Paris.gdb/Transportation"

# Create a Describe object from the network dataset
desc = arcpy.Describe("ParisMultimodal_ND")

justify = 35
print("------- Edge sources")

# Get all the edge sources for the network dataset
edgeSources = desc.edgeSources

if not edgeSources:
    print(" %*s" % (justify, "(No edge sources)"))
    sys.exit(0)

for edgeSource in edgeSources:
    print(" %*s: %s" % (justify, "Source Name" , edgeSource.name))
    print(" %*s: %s" % (justify, "Source ID" , str(edgeSource.sourceID)))
    print(" %*s: %s" % (justify, "Source Type", edgeSource.sourceType))
    print(" %*s: %s" % (justify, "Element Type", edgeSource.elementType))
    print(" %*s: %s" % (justify, "From Elevation Field",
                        edgeSource.fromElevationFieldName))
    print(" %*s: %s" % (justify, "To Elevation Field",
                        edgeSource.toElevationFieldName))
    print(" ")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/edge-source.html#properties

**text**　逐個像元地將兩個網格的值相加(求和)。

**content_version**　3.7

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/arithmetic-plus-operator.html#summary

## + (Addition) operator · 討論

**id**　58d8905cab26a4a8db-2

**english**　+ (Addition) operator

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 運算符
- 算術運算符

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/arithmetic-plus-operator.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/arithmetic-plus-operator.html

**text**　使用具有網格輸入的運算符時，結果將為網格。 但是，如果所有輸入為數字，則結果也將為數字。
當表達式中存在多個運算符時，運算符不一定按照從左到右的順序運行。 具有最高優先級值的運算符將首先運行。 有關詳細信息，請參閱使用 “地圖代數”中的運算符 中的運算符優先級表。 可使用括號來控制運行順序。
使用此運算符時輸入的順序并不重要。
如果兩個輸入值均為整數，則輸出值為整數；否則輸出值為浮點值。
執行“加”運算的另一個方法為 a += b ，其為 a = a + b 的另一種寫法。
如果兩個輸入都是單波段網格，或者輸入之一是常量，則輸出將是單波段網格。
如果兩個輸入都是多波段網格，則運算符將從一個輸入對每個波段執行運算，而輸出將為多波段網格。 每個多波段輸入中的波段數必須相同。
如果輸入之一是多波段網格，而另一個輸入是常量，則該運算符將使用多波段輸入中每個波段的常量值執行預算，而輸出將為多波段網格。
如果兩個輸入均為具有相同數量變量的 多維網格 ，則該運算符將對具有相同維度值的所有剖切片執行運算，并且輸出將為多維網格。 輸入中的變量必須具有相同維度或常見維度，但不能具有不常見的維度。
如果兩個輸入均具有一個變量但名稱不同，請將 匹配多維變量 環境設置為 False 以執行該運算。
如果輸入中的一個是多維網格，而另一個輸入是常量，則運算符將使用常量值對所有變量的所有切片執行運算，并且輸出將為多維網格。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/arithmetic-plus-operator.html#discussion

**text**　# Name: Op_Plus_Ex_02.py
# Description: Adds the values of two rasters on a cell-by-cell basis.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster1 = Raster("cost")
inRaster2 = Raster("degs")

# Run Plus
outPlus = inRaster1 + inRaster2

# Save the output
outPlus.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/outplus")

## HfForward · 匯總

**id**　59ac1aa764c6f1377d-1

**english**　HfForward

- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 水平系數類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html

**text**　通過向前函數定義水平成本系數和水平相對移動角度之間的關系。 該函數規定只允許向前移動。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html#summary

## HfForward · 討論

**id**　59ac1aa764c6f1377d-2

**english**　HfForward

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 水平系數類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html

**text**　此對象用于 距離累積 和 距離分配 Spatial Analyst 工具，以及 路徑距離 、 路徑距離分配 和 路徑距離回溯鏈接 舊的距離工具。
如果某段行程的水平相對移動角度 (HRMA) 小于 45 度，則水平系數 (HF) 將設置為與 zeroFactor 相關的值。 當 HRMA 大于或等于 45 度且小于 90 度時，HF 設置為 sideValue 。 如果 HRMA 大于或等于 90 度，則將 HF 設置為無窮大。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html#discussion

## HfForward · 代碼示例

**id**　59ac1aa764c6f1377d-9

**english**　HfForward

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 水平系數類

**text**　import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"
myHorizFactor = HfForward(0.5, 1.0)
outDistAccum = DistanceAccumulation("source.shp", "barrier.tif",
                                    "elev.tif", "", "", "",
                                    "horizontalRas.tif", myHorizFactor)
outDistAccum.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/distaccumhff")

## HfForward · 代碼示例

**id**　59ac1aa764c6f1377d-11

**english**　HfForward

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 水平系數類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html

**text**　# Name: HfForward_Ex_02.py
# Description: Uses the HfForward object to run the
#              DistanceAccumulation tool.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inSourceData = "sourcepts.shp"
inSurfaceRaster = "elevation.tif"
inHorizontalRaster = "horizontalRas.tif"

# Create the HfForward Object
zeroFactor = 0.5
sideValue = 1.0
myHorizFactor = HfForward(zeroFactor, sideValue)

# Run PathDistance
outDistAccum = DistanceAccumulation(inSourceData,"", inSurfaceRaster,"","",
                                    "", inHorizontalRaster, myHorizFactor)

# Save the output
outDistAccum.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/distaccumhfF2")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/hfforward-class.html#properties

## 加密 Python 工具箱 · 加密 Python 工具箱

**id**　59f9c1c3ebc108217c-1

**english**　Encrypt a Python toolbox

**chapter**　
- Python
- 地理處理和 Python
- 創建地理處理工具
- 在 Python 工具箱中創建工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/encrypting-python-toolboxes.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/encrypting-python-toolboxes.html

**text**　默認情況下，Python 工具箱 (.pyt) 是一個可在任何文本編輯器或 Python IDE 中編輯的純文本文件。 但是，在某些情況下，您可能希望隱藏 Python 工具箱的內容。
要加密 Python 工具箱，請右鍵單擊該工具箱并單擊加密。 輸入并確認密碼時，可保留原始 .pyt 文件的備份副本。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/encrypting-python-toolboxes.html

## 加密 Python 工具箱 · 注意：

**id**　59f9c1c3ebc108217c-2

**english**　Encrypt a Python toolbox

**chapter**　
- Python
- 地理處理和 Python
- 創建地理處理工具
- 在 Python 工具箱中創建工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/encrypting-python-toolboxes.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/encrypting-python-toolboxes.html

**text**　在同一位置對 Python 工具箱執行加密，將覆蓋未加密的原始文件。 建議您對輸入文件進行備份，以免在忘記密碼時造成內容丟失。
要對加密的 Python 工具箱進行解密，請右鍵單擊該工具箱并單擊解密。
此外，ArcPy 函數 EncryptPYT 和 DecryptPYT 也可用于加密或解密 Python 工具箱。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geoprocessing_and_python/encrypting-python-toolboxes.html
