## RadiusVariable · 代碼示例

**id**　5a435b1b2daf941131-11

**english**　RadiusVariable

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 半徑類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/radiusvariable-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/radiusvariable-class.html

**text**　# Name: RadiusVariable_Ex_02.py
# Description: Uses the RadiusVariable object to run IDW tool.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inFeature = "ca_ozone_pts.shp"

# Create the Radius Variable
numberOfPoints = 5
maxDistance = 200000
searchRadius = RadiusVariable(numberOfPoints, maxDistance)

# Run IDW
outIDWRadVar = Idw(inFeature, "elevation", 2000, 3, searchRadius)

# Save the output
outIDWRadVar.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/idwradvar")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/radiusvariable-class.html#properties

## Network Source Directions Field Mapping · 代碼示例

**id**　5ac83acf2995cc5ca0-6

**english**　Network Source Directions Field Mapping

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 網絡資料集屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/network-source-directions-field-mapping.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/network-source-directions-field-mapping.html

**text**　# Name: NDSSourceDirectionsFieldMapping_ex01.py
# Description: Print information about the directions field mappings associated
#              with edge sources in the network dataset.

import arcpy
import sys

network = r"C:/Data/NetworkDatasetWithLandmarks.gdb/Transportation/Streets_ND"

# Create Describe object for the network dataset
desc = arcpy.Describe(network)

#If the directions are not set for the network dataset, exit
if not desc.supportsDirections:
    print("No direction information")
    sys.exit()

# Get all the edge sources
sources = desc.edgeSources

#If there are no edge sources in the network dataset, quit.
if not sources:
    print("No edge sources")
    sys.exit()

#Loop through all the edge sources
for source in sources:
    print("\n--------------------")
    print("Edge source name: " , source.name)
    # Get the directions information specific to this edge source
    sDir = source.sourceDirections
    # Check if the edge source has turn and confirmation landmarks associated with it.
    # If so, print some information about the landmarks.
    if hasattr(sDir, "fieldMappings"):
        fieldMappings = sDir.fieldMappings
        print("\n--Field mapping information--")
        for fm in fieldMappings:
            print("\nProperty key:", fm.key)
            print("Along field name:", fm.alongField)
            print("Against field name:", fm.againstField)
            print("Undirected field name:", fm.undirectedField)
    else:
        print("Source does not directions field mappings.")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/network-source-directions-field-mapping.html#properties

## 在 Python 中驗證表和欄位名稱 · 驗證表名

**id**　5af0654dee3ee3f290-1

**english**　Validating table and field names in Python

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#C0B

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html

**text**　地理資料庫使用各種關系資料庫管理系統 (RDBMS) 來維護組成地理資料庫的許多表。 地理資料庫中的所有表都必須具有有效名稱，因此在地理資料庫中創建資料時，必須要有一種機制檢查表名是否有效。 使用 ValidateTableName() 函數時，可通過腳本確定特定名稱對于特定工作空間而言是否有效。
以下是將要驗證的表名錯誤：
表與資料源的保留字同名（例如，Table）。
表包含無效字符。
表的起始字符無效（例如，使用數字作為第一個字符）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#C0B

**english**　Validating table and field names in Python

**text**　通過將工作空間指定為參數，ArcPy 可以檢查所有現有表名，并確定輸出工作空間是否存在命名限制。 如果輸出工作空間是 RDBMS，則它可能具有不能在表名中使用的保留字。 還可能包含不能在表或欄位名稱中使用的無效字符。 所有無效字符都將替換為下劃線 (_)。 ValidateTableName 可返回表示有效表名的字符串，如果輸入名稱有效，則該字符串可能與輸入名稱相同。 下面的示例可保證由復制圖徵工具創建的新輸出圖徵類具有在任何地理資料庫中都有效的唯一名稱：

**id**　5af0654dee3ee3f290-5

**english**　Validating table and field names in Python

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#C0B

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html

**text**　"""Move all shapefiles from a folder into a geodatabase"""
import arcpy
# Set the workspace. List all of the shapefiles
arcpy.env.workspace = "d:/St_Johns"
fcs = arcpy.ListFeatureClasses("*")
# Set the workspace to SDE for ValidateTableName
arcpy.env.workspace = "Database Connections/Bluestar.sde"
# For each feature class name
for fc in fcs:
 # Validate the output name so it is valid
 outfc = arcpy.ValidateTableName(fc)
 # Copy the features from the workspace to a geodatabase
 arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(fc, outfc)

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#C0B

## 在 Python 中驗證表和欄位名稱 · 驗證欄位名稱

**id**　5af0654dee3ee3f290-6

**english**　Validating table and field names in Python

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#6FD

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html

**text**　每個資料庫都可以對表中的欄位名稱有命名限制。 圖徵類或關系類等對象作為表存儲在 RDBMS 中，因此這些限制影響的不僅僅是獨立表。 這些限制在各種資料庫系統中可能常見，也可能不常見，因此腳本應檢查所有新欄位名稱，以確保工具在執行過程中不會失敗。
以下是將要驗證的欄位名稱錯誤：
欄位與資料源的保留字同名（例如，Table）。
欄位與之前定義的欄位同名。
欄位包含無效字符（例如，*）。
欄位名稱超出資料源的最大欄位名稱長度。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#6FD

## 在 Python 中驗證表和欄位名稱 · 驗證欄位名稱

**id**　5af0654dee3ee3f290-9

**english**　Validating table and field names in Python

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#6FD

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html

**text**　"""
Create a new numeric field containing the ratio of polygon area to
polygon perimeter. Two arguments, a feature class and field name,
are expected.
"""
# Define a pair of simple exceptions for error handling
class ShapeError(Exception):
 pass
class FieldError(Exception):
 pass
import arcpy
import os
try:
 # Get the input feature class and make sure it contains polygons
 input = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(0)
 desc = arcpy.Describe(input)
 if desc.shapeType.lower() != "polygon":
     raise ShapeError
 # Get the new field name and validate it
 fieldname = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(1)
 fieldname = arcpy.ValidateFieldName(fieldname, os.path.dirname(input))
 # Make sure shape_length and shape_area fields exist
 if len(arcpy.ListFields(input, "Shape_area")) > 0 and \
     len(arcpy.ListFields(input, "Shape_length")) > 0:
     # Add the new field and calculate the value
     arcpy.management.AddField(input, fieldname, "double")
     arcpy.management.CalculateField(input, fieldname,
                                     "[Shape_area] / [Shape_length]")
 else:
     raise FieldError
except ShapeError:
 print("Input does not contain polygons")
except FieldError:
 print("Input does not contain shape area and length fields")
except arcpy.ExecuteError:
 print(arcpy.GetMessages(2))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/working-with-geodatabases.html#6FD

## CheckOutExtension · 許可：

**id**　5afab3ee1d3c74664a-2

**english**　CheckOutExtension

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 許可和安裝

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/checkoutextension.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/checkoutextension.html

**text**　僅在使用浮動版許可的情況下需要 CheckOutExtension ；對于所有其他情況，它沒有任何影響。 使用浮動版許可時，腳本檢索到擴展模塊許可后，即可使用擴展模塊工具。 腳本使用完擴展模塊中的工具后，應使用 CheckInExtension 函數歸還許可，以便其他應用程序使用。 當腳本完成時，將歸還所有檢出的擴展模塊許可和設置的產品許可。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/checkoutextension.html#summary

- List

outReclassRV.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/reclassrv")

- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊

- 重映射類

**text**　# Name: RemapValue_Ex_02.py
# Description: Uses the RemapValue object to run Reclassify tool.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "negs"

# Define the RemapValue Object
myRemapVal = RemapValue([[-3,9],[0,1],[3,-4],[4,5],[5,6],[6,4],[7,-7]])

# Run Reclassify
outReclassRV = Reclassify(inRaster, "VALUE", myRemapVal, "")

# Save the output
outReclassRV.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/reclassrevar2")

進度對話框中顯示的地理處理錯誤編號是指向進一步描述錯誤的幫助頁面的超鏈接。 要啟用腳本中的錯誤的超鏈接，請使用 AddReturnMessage 函數，而不是 AddError 函數，如下所示：

## 地理編碼共享草案 · 摘要摘要

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-1

**english**　GeocodingSharingDraft

**chapter**　
- Python 符號
- 共享模塊
- 類類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html

**text**　地理編碼共享草稿類允許您創建一個服務定義文件草案(.sdgraft), 以共享定位器作為定位項目, 由地理編碼服務驅動, 或者將定位器作為批量地理代碼服務共享, 這在發布時作為網絡工具項目出現, 由地球處理服務驅動。 所有地理編碼服務都需要一個定位器。 該定位器是進行地址匹配所需的所有資料的主要地理編碼工具。 在 ArcGIS Pro 中, 使用創建定位器或創建功能定位器來創建定位器。 一旦創建了定位器, 您可以將其公布在活動中的 ARCGIS 企業門戶網站上。 根據自身能力, 一個定位器。具有地理編碼能力的定位器將作為地理編碼服務和定位設備在門戶網站上創建的定位設備進行發布。具有批次地理編碼能力的定位器將作為批次地理編碼服務進行發布。作為批次地理編碼服務出版的定位器將作為一個網絡工具項目在您的ArcGIS Enterprise門戶網站上由地球處理服務提供動力。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The GeocodingSharingDraft class allows you to create a service definition draft file (.sddraft) to share a locator as a locator item powered by a geocode service or share a locator as a batch geocode service, which appears as a web tool item powered by a geoprocessing service when published.\nAll geocode services require a locator. The locator is the main tool for geocoding and contains all the data necessary to perform address matching. In ArcGIS Pro, use the Create Locator or Create Feature Locator tool to create a locator. Once you create a locator, you can publish it on your active ArcGIS Enterprise portal. Depending on its capabilities, a locator is published as one of the following service types:\nLocators with geocoding capabilities will be published as geocode services and power the locator items created in the portal.\nLocators with batch geocoding capabilities will be published as batch geocode services. A locator published as a batch geocode service will appear as a web tool item powered by a geoprocessing service on your ArcGIS Enterprise portal.", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#summary

## 地理編碼共享草案 · 摘要摘要

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-2

**english**　GeocodingSharingDraft

**chapter**　
- Python 符號
- 共享模塊
- 類類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html

**text**　在ArcGIS Enterprise 中,作為這些服務之一的出版定位器是您如何向組織內的每一個人提供地理編碼。互聯網或內聯網用戶然后可以使用網絡應用程序、ArcGIS Pro、ArcGIS Online和其他客戶應用程序的服務。在共享定位器之前,有幾種辦法可在服務器上管理定位資料,以供考慮。如果定位器存放在特定地點,或者應該儲存在服務器上——例如,如果定位器大小大于1GB,則將文件夾位置登記為資料存儲處。使用資料庫可以最大限度地減少重寫或覆蓋服務時的服務中斷時間。如果定位器存放在本地機器上,則發布程序。復制定位器到服務器。 見以下主題, 以便了解更多關于管理定位器資料的信息: 讓 ArcGIS 服務器在向ArcGIS 服務器發布時可以訪問資料 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Publishing a locator as one of these services in ArcGIS Enterprise is how you make geocoding available to everyone in your organization. Internet or intranet users can then use the services in web apps, ArcGIS Pro, ArcGIS Online, and other client applications.\nBefore sharing a locator, there are a few options for managing locator data on the server to consider. If the locator is, or should be, stored on the server in a specific location—for example, if the locator is larger than 1 GB in size—register the folder location as a data store. Using a data store can minimize service downtime when restarting or overwriting a service. If the locator is stored on a local machine, the publishing process copies the locator to the server. See the following topics to learn more about managing locator data:\nMake data accessible to ArcGIS Server\nCopying data when publishing to ArcGIS Server", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#summary

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html

**text**　要創建地理編碼共享草案對象,請使用 Create sharing Shaling 起草功能或直接使用關鍵參數創建一個地理編碼分享目標對象。要將一個定位器作為ArcGIS企業的地理代碼服務,將服務器類型參數設置為 Federedaled_SERVER ,將服務類型參數設置為 GEOCODE_SERVICE 。要將一個定位器作為獨立的 ArcGIS服務器的地理代碼服務發布為 獨立ArcGIS 服務器,將服務器類型參數設置為STANDALONE_SERVERVER 和Gocode_SERVICE的服務類型參數。要將定位器作為批次地理代碼服務設置為ArcGIS企業,將服務器類型參數設置為 FEDEReded和 Batchch_GEOCODE_SERVICE 的服務類型參數。在配置 GeocodingSharing Splain Gift 對象后,可以使用導出ToSDB草案方法將其保存到服務定義文件草稿(.sddrop)中,然后可以使用階段服務和上傳服務定義工具將其制成并共享給ArcGIS Enterprises或ArcGIS服務器。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "To create a GeocodingSharingDraft object, use the CreateSharingDraft function or create a GeocodingSharingDraft object directly using keyword arguments.\nTo publish a locator as a geocode service to ArcGIS Enterprise, set the serverType parameter to FEDERATED_SERVER and the serviceType parameter to GEOCODE_SERVICE.\nTo publish a locator as a geocode service to a stand-alone ArcGIS Server, set the serverType parameter to STANDALONE_SERVER and the serviceType parameter to GEOCODE_SERVICE.\nTo publish a locator as a batch geocode service to ArcGIS Enterprise, set the serverType parameter to FEDERATED_SERVER and the serviceType parameter to BATCH_GEOCODE_SERVICE.\nAfter the GeocodingSharingDraft object is configured, it can be saved to a service definition draft file (.sddraft) using the exportToSDDraft method. It can then be staged and shared to either ArcGIS Enterprise or ArcGIS Server using the Stage Service and Upload Service Definition tools.", "section": "Discussion"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#discussion

## 地理編碼共享草案 · 屬性屬性

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-6

**parameter**　capabilities

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 能力(讀寫)
- 指定服務所支持的能力。參數應指定為包含下列關鍵字之一或數個的列表:FUD_ADIDES_CANDIDATES - 請注明查找地址操作是允許的。 GEOCODE - 標明查找地址或地理編碼一個地址表是允許的。當離線目標值為11.4或更久時使用GECODE。 GECODE_ADINSES - 注明地址表的地理編碼是允許的。 RWERSE_GECODE - 注明反向地理編碼操作是允許的。 SUGGEST - 注明建議操作是允許的。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["capabilities (Read and Write)", "Specifies the capabilities a service will support. The parameter should be specified as a list containing one or more of the following string keywords: FIND_ADDRESS_CANDIDATES—Specifies find address operations are allowed. GEOCODE—Specifies finding addresses or geocoding a table of addresses is allowed. Use GEOCODE when offlineTarget is 11.4 or older. GEOCODE_ADDRESSES—Specifies geocoding a table of addresses is allowed. REVERSE_GEOCODE—Specifies reverse geocoding operations are allowed. SUGGEST—Specifies suggest operations are allowed.", "String"], "section": "Properties"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-7

- 類類

**parameter**　copyDataToServer

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 復制 DataToServer( 讀和寫)
- 指定定位器中引用的資料是否會被復制到服務器。 True - 將復制結果中的所有資料,包括已注冊到服務器上的資料。錯誤 - 僅復制未注冊到服務器上的資料。服務處將引用在聯合服務器上登記的資料。默認值為 False。
- 布爾

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["copyDataToServer (Read and Write)", "Specifies whether the data referenced in the locator will be copied to the server. True—All data in the result will be copied, including data that is registered with the server. False—Only data that is not registered with the server will be copied. Data registered with the federated server will be referenced by the service. The default value is False.", "Boolean"], "section": "Properties"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-15

**english**　GeocodingSharingDraft

**parameter**　maxUsageTime

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 最大用戶時間(讀寫)
- 客戶端使用服務的最大時間( 秒) 。 GEOCODE_ SERVICE 的默認值是600s (約10分鐘)。 BATCH_ GEOCODE_SERVICE 的默認值是86000( 約24小時) 。 對長期地理編碼工作使用更大的值。 減少這一時間以確保客戶不會誤用服務 。
- 整數

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["maxUsageTime (Read and Write)", "The maximum time, in seconds, a client can use a service. The default value for GEOCODE_SERVICE is 600s (approximately 10 minutes). The default value for BATCH_GEOCODE_SERVICE is 86000 (approximately 24 hours). Use a larger value for long-running geocoding jobs. Reduce this time to ensure that a client will not misapply the service.", "Integer"], "section": "Properties"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

- 指定是否保存離線服務定義。如果使用該類導出ToSD草案方法創建了連接的服務定義草案文件(.sdgraft),將錯誤使用。如果服務器類型屬性設為 Federed_SERVER,請在門戶網站上簽名。如果服務器類型屬性設為STANDALONE_SERVER,則向該類目標服務器屬性提供服務器 URL或服務器連接文件(.ags)。如果使用該類導出 ToSD草案方法創建了一個離線服務定義草案文件(.sdgraft)。如果服務器類型屬性設為FEDER,則無需在門戶網站上簽名。如果服務器類型屬性被設置為 STANDALONE_SERVER, 或提供服務器連接文件或 URL 。 默認值為 False 。
- 布爾

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["offline (Read and Write)", "Specifies whether an offline service definition will be saved. False—Use if a connected service definition draft file (.sddraft) is created using the exportToSDDraft method of the class. If the serverType property is set to FEDERATED_SERVER, sign in to a portal. If the serverType property is set to STANDALONE_SERVER, provide a server URL or a server connection file (.ags) to the targetServer property of the class. True—Use if an offline service definition draft file (.sddraft) is created using the exportToSDDraft method of the class. There is no need to sign in to a portal if the serverType property is set to FEDERATED_SERVER, or to provide a server connection file or URL if the serverType property is set to STANDALONE_SERVER. The default value is False.", "Boolean"], "section": "Properties"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

## 地理編碼共享草案 · 屬性屬性

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-19

**english**　GeocodingSharingDraft

**chapter**　
- Python 符號
- 共享模塊
- 類類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html

**parameter**　offlineTarget

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 離線目標( 讀和寫)
- 指定發布服務定義的目標服務器版本。指定一個版本確保服務定義包含與您的服務器兼容的內容。該屬性只有在離線屬性設定為 True 時才被維護。 您可以以下列方式使用此屬性: 版本整數 - 使用服務器版本的確切數字, 而不是指定目標服務器版本。 如果服務類型設置為 Batch_ GEOCODE_SERVICE 和將離線屬性設置為 True, 您必須使用版本整數 。 12.12- 380 10x- Content 將與 ArcGIS Enterprise 10. 9. 1 或更早的匹配。 11 - 當出版服務器與ArcGIS Server 11. 0 或以后兼容時使用。 這是默認值 。GECODE_SERVICE 僅支持12.2版或以后的版本。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["offlineTarget (Read and Write)", "Specifies the target server version to which the service definition will be published. Specifying a version ensures that the service definition contains content compatible with your server. This property is only honored when the offline property is set to True. You can use this property in the following ways: Version integer number—Use the exact number of the server version rather than specifying the target server version. If the serviceType is set to BATCH_GEOCODE_SERVICE and the offline property is set to True, you must use the version integer number. 12.2—380 10x—Content will be compatible with ArcGIS Enterprise 10.9.1 or earlier. 11—Use when the publishing server is compatible with ArcGIS Server 11.0 or later. This is the default. BATCH_GEOCODE_SERVICE only supports version 12.2 or later.", "String"], "section": "Properties"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

## 地理編碼共享草案 · 屬性屬性

**id**　5b6a7c72efee9c01b6-31

**english**　GeocodingSharingDraft

**chapter**　
- Python 符號
- 共享模塊
- 類類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/sharing/geocodingsharingdraft-class.html

**parameter**　targetServer

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 目標服務員(閱讀和寫)
- 將發布服務的服務器 。 當離線屬性設置為 False 時, 此屬性必須設定為默認值 。 當服務器類型屬性設為 FEDEREADI_ SERVER 時, 請向 ArcGIS Enterport 連接服務器提供服務器 URL 。 當服務器類型屬性設為 SSTANDALONE_ SERVER 時, 支持以下功能: ArcGIS 服務器連接 - 您可以使用ArcGIS Pro工程窗口服務器節點下列出的 ARCGIS 服務器連接, 或者您可以瀏覽到服務器連接文件(.ags) 存儲的文件夾。 ArcGIS 服務器的 URL - 您可以指定ArcGIS 服務器的 URL, 只要有 ArCGIS 服務器的管理員或出版商鏈接, 您也可以指定ArcGIS 服務器的 URL加入ArcGISPro項目。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["targetServer (Read and Write)", "The server where the service will be published. This property must be set when the offline property is set to False, which is the default. When the serverType property is set to FEDERATED_SERVER, provide the server URL to the ArcGIS Enterprise portal federated server. When the serverType property is set to STANDALONE_SERVER, the following are supported: ArcGIS Server connection—You can use ArcGIS Server connections listed under the Servers node in the ArcGIS Pro Project window, or you can browse to a folder where server connection files (.ags) are stored. URL to ArcGIS Server—You can specify the URL to ArcGIS Server, provided an administrator or publisher connection to ArcGIS Server has been added to the ArcGIS Pro project.", "String"], "section": "Properties"}

**text**　在進行服務之前分析服務定義草案。 方法可以返回一些錯誤和警告, 如缺少元資料、 復制資料到服務器或不兼容參數。 在成功運行導出 ToSDGavorpt 方法后使用此方法。 如果沒有生成服務定義草案文件, 嘗試使用此方法時會發生錯誤 。

**original**　{"text": "Analyzes the service definition draft before staging a service. The method can return a number of errors and warnings, such as for missing metadata, copying data to the server, or incompatible parameters.\nUse this method after running the exportToSDDraft method successfully. If there is no service definition draft file generated, there will be an error when trying to use this method.", "section": "analyzeSDDraft()"}
